PREFACE 


This brochure contains an Index to the Gazette of India 
for the month of January, 1957. Notifications relating to Acts, 
Ordinance and Regulations (Part II, Section 1) and Statutory 
Rules and Orders (Part II, Seections 3 and 4) have been indexed 
exhaustivly. As regards other Notifications (Part I, Sections 1 
and 3, Part III Section 1 only items of importance from Mem¬ 
bers’ viewpoint have been indexed. References to the Parts and 
Sections of Gazette along with the page numbers have been 
given in the index for facilty of reference. 

New Delhi ; M. N. KAUL, 

The 15th July, 1957. Secretary.

PREFACE 


This brochure contains an Index to the Gazette of India for 
the month of February, 1957. Notifications relating to Acts 
Ordinances and Regulations (Part II, Section 1) and Statutory 
Rules and Orders (Part II, Sections 3 and 4) have been indexed 
exhaustively. As regards other Notifications (Part I, Sections 1 
and 3; Part III, Section 1) only items of importance from 
Members’ viewpoint have been indexed. References to the Parts 
and Sections of Gazette along with the page numbers have been 
given in the index for facility of reference. 


New Delhi ; M. N. KAUL, 

The 21st September , 1957 - Secretary.
